## OPEN FINDINGS

Existing **WPP01-CR-001 remains open**: inventory eligibility and identity are not one shared contract. P02/P03 show the extension-case deletion false pass; K02/K08 and Z04 show nested state and duplicate work-package records are invisible or accepted; F01 shows a tracked directory reaches a file-only operation. U12 separates physical case sensitivity from Git's exact filename identity. Treat these as one inventory characterization neighborhood, not repeated isolated fixes.

Existing **WPP01-CR-002 remains open**: the object established by an earlier check is not bound to the object used by subsequent proof. A01/A02/A03 reproduce namespace substitution. O02 accepts an annotated-tag object as the candidate commit. Q01/Q02 turn canonical orphan mains into apparent ancestors using replacement/graft metadata. A06/F04/F05 demonstrate mutable-ref and mutable-file check/use separation. F06 accepts a merge in the contract's linear state-only chain. J01/J02 accept registry filename lookalikes as allowed state-only changes. J05 follows a symbolic head into tags; J06–J08 roundtrip matching tag, abbreviated-SHA and remote inputs through the branch-shaped lexical gate. These are independently executed witnesses, not conclusions from Claude's response record. A05's simpler replacement experiment did **not** produce a false pass; Q01's virtual-parent experiment did. That distinction must survive classification.

New **WPP01-CR-003 — malformed-input totality is incomplete.** S052/S053 and X103/X104 execute unhashable owners; F01 and K06 execute non-file/dangling state reads. Direct/CLI entry points can raise rather than return their documented invalid-state result; `run_all` sometimes converts the same failure to `internal error`. Proposed class: SEMANTICS_NEUTRAL_HARDENING where failure already blocks action, CONTRACT_ASSURANCE_DEFECT if a coordinator mistakes an exception/partial result for completion. Claude must classify the concrete rows, not infer that every exception is a false approval. Windows setup failures on unrepresentable filenames are **not** witnesses for this finding.

New **WPP01-CR-004 — derived-source output path is not bounded by its key.** Z06 executes `sources build` with key `../escaped-marker`, returns exit 0, and writes outside `reference/source-text` (still entirely within its disposable fixture). `cli.py:59` is the write boundary. Proposed class: CONTRACT_ASSURANCE_DEFECT; proposed priority: high. Required policy is bounded derived output, not an implementation prescription. Z09's hash mismatch is a useful opposite control: it refuses the write. Input authority does not make an unchecked output path a valid reading-aid identifier.

New **WPP01-CR-005 — control-state evidence uses permissive tokens/truthiness beyond declared shapes.** Q03 accepts floating-point schema version 2.0; Q08/Q09/Q10 accept non-ASCII digits or a trailing newline in finding IDs; convergence episode/open_findings rows accept truthy wrong types; N01/N02 non-actionable next-action cases accept an action string while the status says no action is authorized. Proposed class: CONTRACT_ASSURANCE_DEFECT for operational routing/evidence fields, SEMANTICS_NEUTRAL_HARDENING or REQUIREMENT_UNCERTAIN for unused display metadata. These distinctions are deliberately proposed for the challenge, not declared agreed. The exact ASCII ID and optional-metadata policy requires a recorded checkpoint decision.

No closure is claimed for any finding. No new finding file or state record was written: this single characterization artifact records the new IDs for the coordinator's next pass.

## ROOT-CAUSE SURFACE

The validator combines host-dependent filesystem selection, raw Git inventories, YAML objects, mutable names, canonical-looking OIDs, and ambient Git graph/configuration as though each describes the same snapshot and authority. Some gates validate shape, some validate a namespace, some peel or re-resolve objects, and some reread a file; their successful results are not composed into one stable proof. The required characterization therefore separates path eligibility from path identity, object existence from exact object type, local branch existence from branch-tip binding, ordinary Git graph views from canonical ancestry, semantic equality from byte equality, mechanical transition legality from evidence authorization, and diagnostic failure from semantic rejection.

## OBSERVABLE RULES

These are **proposed checkpoint rules**, not permission to implement. Existing explicit requirements remain binding; choices that refine an unspecified boundary must be classified by Claude and agreed at the checkpoint.

1. **R1 — repository and exact-object binding.** Each candidate side uses its designated repository, and each declared root is verified before repository-dependent proof. Ambient Git repository/object selectors must not substitute another repository for `-C`. A candidate SHA is the exact lowercase 40-hex OID of a **commit object**, not an annotated-tag OID that peels to a commit. CLOSED proofs concern canonical `refs/heads/main` history in both repositories. Decide explicitly whether replacement refs/grafts are refused or bypassed; they must not fabricate acceptance. Packed/symbolic local refs may be supported only through the same stable local-branch proof. Canonical graph semantics are a proposed strengthening of “accepted onto main”; the Q witnesses make that choice explicit.
2. **R2 — one inventory eligibility contract.** Working and committed state inventories use the same eligibility and path-kind policy on every host. Proposed canonical state domain: immediate regular UTF-8-named files with literal lowercase `.yaml`, one file per work package; reserved/unsupported variants in the state area are reported rather than silently becoming additional authority. The checkpoint must choose whether alternate-case extensions are rejected or included consistently; neither choice permits a deletion to disappear on only one side. Matching inventories, uncommitted extras, working deletions, and legitimate committed deletions are separate cases. Nested state, directories, symlinks and gitlinks must be explicitly admitted with defined semantics or refused. Do not conflate inventory equality with state validity.
3. **R3 — exact path identity and transport.** Git path records are raw NUL-framed strict UTF-8, with no trim, line split, display-unquoting, casefold or Unicode normalization. Eligibility is applied after exact decoding. Preserve quote/tab/newline/leading-space/non-ASCII names in comparisons and errors; an unrepresentable host name must fail closed without loss of its committed identity. Distinct case or normalization spellings in Git remain distinct even on a host that cannot materialize both. Working type and committed mode must agree with the declared inventory policy. Other inventories (findings/spec/derived sources/conformance) need their own explicit domains; sharing a parser does not make their eligibility rules identical.
4. **R4 — stable proof snapshot.** Resolve the qualified local branch once to an immutable commit OID; use that OID throughout ancestry and descendant proofs. Resolve roundtrip HEAD/local-branch/full-SHA input once; use that commit for both inventory and every blob. Never validate one namespace and use a bare ambiguous name. A state object validated at the load boundary must be the object used by downstream checks; reread changes require failure/retry or an explicit new proof, not silent mixing. State-only active descendants form the declared **linear** chain; per-commit allowed-path checking remains mandatory, including changes later reverted. CLOSED keeps its accepted-main exemption after canonical acceptance is proven.
5. **R5 — exact operational shapes.** YAML core rejection of duplicate keys/aliases/tags/non-JSON values remains before any business logic. No implicit str/bool/int conversion, numeric equality in place of token type, truthiness in place of required evidence, or regex end-anchor in place of a complete token. Proposed schema_version is integer 2; work_package is a nonempty stable string whose filename/uniqueness policy is decided with R2; list fields contain complete declared ID tokens (proposed ASCII digits). Candidate maps contain only code/docs; side records have exact repo/ref/sha strings. Active pre-candidate null/absent sides can remain valid; CLOSED cannot. Optional display/history/authority metadata must either have declared shapes or remain opaque **and confer no authority**. Do not tighten unused metadata accidentally without classification.
6. **R6 — status-specific evidence and routing.** Every active state requires the permitted owner and nonempty action; BLOCKED_FOR_OWNER requires Owner. WAITING_FOR_TRIGGER requires a real declared trigger and null owner/action. CONTRACT_CONVERGENCE requires a declared episode string and a nonempty list of finding IDs, not truthy booleans/numbers/text. Non-actionable states authorize no owner or action. Quarantine permits no agent owner. CLOSED requires complete accepted pair, no open findings, and either exact APPROVED process-review evidence or product CERTIFIED_CLOSED registry evidence. The positive-label guard and UTF-8 safe roundtrip are preserved. Stored review/governance strings alone do not establish independence, acceptance authority or fired triggers; those remain explicit coordinator/agent duties.
7. **R7 — fail closed with a complete diagnostic.** Malformed YAML/type/path/object/transport inputs produce a documented error result and CLI failure, not an uncaught exception, partial success, or success-empty inventory. CLI usage failures remain usage failures. Setup inability on a host is diagnostic-only evidence, not a killed semantic negative control. The aggregate check may catch errors, but direct entry points must obey their advertised invalid-state/error contract too. No dependent action follows a failed/exceptional roundtrip.
8. **R8 — CLI and producer authority boundaries.** Parsed args, a selected check, validate-only success, roundtrip equality, and a legal transition edge each prove only their advertised property. None grants acceptance. Explicit/default path contexts must be verified before assurance use. Source-build inputs remain hash-verified and source authority is separate from derived aids; derived keys/output paths cannot escape their declared destinations. Invalid source entries and output failures yield diagnostics; decide whether writes are staged or preflighted so a failed build cannot leave a misleading partial success. Optional `--docs` on validate must not be described as a containment check unless it actually becomes one.

## BEHAVIOR MATRIX

The matrix uses finite **equivalence partitions**, not a claim to enumerate an infinite string space. It covers every audited consumer and its distinct branch conditions, then crosses the interacting boundaries: matching/deletion/committed-deletion × filename/host; candidate code/docs × repo/ref/SHA × malformed shape; status × owner/action/candidate/evidence; local existence × later namespace use × active/CLOSED; Git/raw-byte failure × inventory/roundtrip; and controlled mutation × verification/use. Literal repetition of an unrelated opaque field with every Git namespace would not add a resolution path. Claude must challenge this partition argument and identify any omitted interacting class before the checkpoint.

Every current cell is an executed result from the unchanged modules. `RETURN []`/`RETURN {}` means the invoked error-returning gate reported no error, **not project approval**. CLI cells retain exit/output. `RAISED` is retained rather than relabelled “rejected”. Results from unsupported filename setup do not establish downstream validator semantics. Mode `INJECTED_IO` executes the actual frozen helper against controlled raw transport; `INJECTED_RACE…` mutates only scratch files/refs after the stated real call; `INJECTED_ENV_WITH_REAL_GIT` uses real Git with a scoped environment. These modes are not claims of naturally occurring races or native malformed-transport production.

W = Windows/NTFS; L = Linux on the E: Drvfs mount (case-insensitive filesystem); C = Linux on a project-contained ext4 image (case-sensitive). H07 proves the distinction by reading separate A/a values. Logs and harnesses are below; current results are embedded here durably. Disposable absolute fixture roots, distinct OIDs and error text are retained in the raw current JSON. The required-behavior column names R1–R8; its input/case and witness identify which clause applies. A passing baseline is an acceptance control, malformed/setup rows may be diagnostic controls, and not every row kills every listed wrong implementation. Claude must classify each row and state its final accept/reject/diagnostic-only oracle rather than using today's behavior as the required oracle.

**Executed catalog:** 688 input/case rows; 2064 recorded host cells. Counts exclude the many successful fixture baselines run before each mutation. All three hosts executed the complete ID set.

## NEGATIVE CONTROLS

Each matrix witness is a candidate control against its stated wrong implementation. The controls below are the **minimum discriminating groups** for the one agreed implementation; Claude's classification must also retain every other semantic row as a positive/negative control or give its explicit diagnostic/deferred reason. Setup exceptions and equality-only checks cannot count as semantic kills. Require the expected error category/property, not incidental fixture SHAs or exact wording. Check a known-bad version actually survives the negative witness where claimed and the agreed implementation kills it; the good baseline must continue to pass. A manufactured malformed transport is a unit control, not a native-host result.

| Control group | Rows | Wrong implementation it distinguishes | Required opposite control |
|---|---|---|---|
| Shared state eligibility | P01–P03 a/b/c, U12 | host glob paired with literal Git suffix; casefolding Git names | matching canonical inventory and legitimate committed deletion pass; alternate extension policy is explicit on both hosts |
| Inventory completeness and identity | P04–P16, K02/K08, Z04, D01–D04 | only compare surviving working files; ignore nested/duplicate/unsupported committed records | canonical one-WP-per-file passes; unsupported setup is diagnostic, raw Git-tree omission is a real negative |
| Object-kind handling | K01/K05–K07, F01/F08 | follow arbitrary links; read directories; confuse bare/root contexts | regular tracked state passes; invalid type produces a documented failure |
| Raw path transport | H01–H06, I01–I07, D*, U05–U10 | display quoting, trimming, splitlines, lossy decoding, normalization | exact UTF-8/NUL identities preserved; native config variants and injected framing remain separately labelled |
| Candidate side and object | candidate S*, O01–O06 | side-selected arbitrary repository; peeled tag accepted as exact commit | actual commit on the fixed side passes; blob/missing/tag/malformed OID fails under agreed R1/R5 |
| Namespace and object binding | G*, A01–A04, M05/M06, J05–J08 | checked heads but used tag/bare revision; abbreviated/remote revision accepted as plain branch | qualified ordinary local head, allowed symbolic-head policy, packed refs and explicit commit revision continue to work |
| Canonical acceptance graph | Q01/Q02 versus A05/A04 | replacement/graft graph fabricates main acceptance | true main ancestry passes; orphan canonical mains fail even if another namespace/graph view suggests ancestry |
| Snapshot races | A06, F04/F05 | resolve mutable ref or reread unvalidated state between proof/use | one fixed commit/object snapshot passes; a changed input cannot be silently credited as the prior proof |
| State-only chain | U01–U11, F06, J01–J04 | endpoint-only diff, prefix lookalike, merge accepted as linear chain | actual linear permitted-path descendants pass; registry filename equality is distinct from directory prefixes |
| Structural/core load boundary | Y01–Y18, S*, X*, Q03–Q10 | parser collapsing duplicate keys, truthy/numeric/type/token coercion | strict valid core YAML and exact operational tokens pass; permitted opaque metadata stays non-authoritative |
| Status evidence and routing | per-status S*, Z01–Z03, N01–N08, U13–U17 | active/CLOSED exemptions conflated; legal edge mistaken for Owner authority or fired trigger | valid process CLOSED/product-certified/deferred/blocked states retain their specific controls; edge legality alone proves no evidence |
| Totality and process failure | S052/S053, X103/X104, F01/F02, K06, I08/I09, M07/M08 | uncaught type/IO error, success-empty on failed Git | valid baseline passes; all public invalid input exits/results are documented failures |
| Raw state equality | T01–T05, B02 | semantic-only equality hides comment/byte delta; locale decoding; broad newline normalization | CRLF transport-only equality passes; changed scalar/comment/invalid bytes fail at the advertised gate |
| CLI scope/context | C*, V*, Z10, F03/F08 | empty `--only`, validate-only, wrong root or usage success interpreted as full assurance | valid selected command succeeds for its stated scope; unselected properties are not claimed checked |
| Source producer boundary | Z06–Z09, E12, C16 | unbounded derived key; malformed entry/coercion; hash bypass/partial output | canonical bounded key and valid hash succeed; attempted escape/hash mismatch fails before unauthorized output |

Positive-label WP00-CR-014, explicit UTF-8 decoding, and the CLOSED accepted-main exemption are retained constraints. No matrix result reopens their established concepts. F04 challenges **which immutable commit** is decoded, not UTF-8; A03/Q01/Q02 challenge **which main/graph** supplies ancestry, not whether a legitimately accepted CLOSED candidate is exempt. There is no blanket assertion that the old tooling fails every new row; controls must be labelled discriminating, positive, or diagnostic from their actual replay.

## IMPLEMENTATION CONSTRAINTS

These are behavioral constraints, not a patch or implementation plan approved at this pass.

- Agree the eligibility, object-kind, symbolic-ref, canonical-graph, revision-domain, exact-token and optional-metadata policies **before** changing the frozen surface. Record each matrix row's classification and final oracle, including legitimate acceptance and diagnostic-only rows.
- Establish one immutable proof context for the selected repository, exact commit and loaded state. Reuse it for dependent comparisons. A successful existence query is not a snapshot; a branch-shaped string is not a local-head resolution; a peeled object is not an exact commit OID.
- Bind root verification to repository-dependent queries; explicitly handle ambient Git selectors and graph overlays. Do not cure tag collisions alone while leaving another resolution path with bare names or virtual ancestry.
- Define Git/working inventory eligibility together, with exact identities and kinds. Report unsupported/colliding host representations. Keep directory prefix membership separate from the single allowed registry filename.
- Preserve per-commit path checking and enforce the declared linear chain for active state-only descendants. Preserve the CLOSED exemption after the actual accepted pair is proven on canonical mains.
- Keep parsing, byte equality, inventory equality, structural validation, status evidence, edge legality, agent independence, and acceptance authority as separately named proof obligations. A limited command must report its limited scope honestly.
- Ensure direct APIs and CLI entry points return documented errors for malformed input. Aggregate internal-error handling is not a substitute for a total direct gate. Never count exception-only controls as semantic rejection without checking coordinator behavior.
- Keep source-bundle hashes and non-authoritative derived aids separate. A manifest key cannot choose an arbitrary output destination. A failed producer must not advertise successful derivation or leave outputs mistaken for a complete build.
- Ship controls for the agreed equivalence classes on Windows and a genuinely case-sensitive POSIX filesystem. Preserve raw framing/unit controls as separately identified tests. Use exact expected semantics, not current errors as the new oracle.
- Production edits remain frozen until Claude's proposed classification and Codex's separate approval establish AGREED FOR IMPLEMENTATION. Claude implements once; Codex then performs targeted closure. This artifact neither closes findings nor starts WP-01.

## OUT-OF-SCOPE / DEFERRED

| Item | Reason | Trigger |
|---|---|---|
| WP-01 product/Rust/Python contract or implementation; `review-worktrees/docs-wp01` | G-019 permits Claude's isolated drafting, not this review; G-018 task is validator characterization | WP-P01 closure and explicit WP-01 handoff |
| Production patches, state/finding-file edits, implementation/checkpoint approval | task is characterization; frozen surface and coordinator ownership | completed challenge, row classification and separate two-party checkpoint |
| Owner acceptance / source semantic adjudication | mechanical validator evidence does not supply governance authority or decide source meaning | actual governed transition or source conflict |
| macOS/APFS/HFS normalization and locale-specific filesystems | actual hosts are Windows/NTFS, Linux/Drvfs and Linux/ext4; setting `core.precomposeunicode` is not a macOS filesystem probe | support of such a host, or a proposed normalization rule requiring its evidence |
| SHA-256 repository OIDs | current contract explicitly names 40-hex SHA-1 commits; changing repository format is not a validator hardening inference | Owner-authorized repository-format change |
| Host resource exhaustion, arbitrary permission matrices, filesystem/OS fault fuzzing, every possible concurrent interleaving | matrix covers semantic input equivalence classes, concrete IO failures and deliberate proof/use witnesses; these are additional transport/runtime fault campaigns, not infinite Cartesian rows | implementation uses a new IO/locking/permission boundary or a discovered unrepresented failure class |
| Compromised Git executable/library, bytecode, OS or malicious process already authorized to overwrite the validator | no software gate can prove itself against arbitrary runtime replacement; fixture source fingerprints and canonical graph/ambient selector probes bound the modeled surface | changed execution/provenance threat model |
| Source DOCX/MD contents, derivation fidelity, product citation meaning beyond frozen provenance/path consumer boundaries | authority and content were not mutated; source producer and inventory consumers are characterized here | a source/provenance change or derivation defect witness |
| Extra literal repetitions of independent opaque metadata × every unrelated ref namespace | no additional field-to-Git/comparison path; sources inventory explicitly records the separation | Claude identifies an interaction, newly promoted metadata authority, or changed consumer |

The deferred entries are explicit limits, not claims of proof. In-scope namespaces, path classes and operational shapes have executed cells; host setup inability is recorded in those cells instead of being deferred silently. Any completeness challenge adds a named row/class and must be resolved before the checkpoint.
